            "description": "<p style=\"margin:0px;color:#10182899\">Ready to take the next step in your fitness journey?             "deleted": false,
            "description": "<p style=\"margin:0px;color:#10182899\">A personalised 30–45 minute consultation to deeply understand your goals, needs, and preferences. This call is designed to set the foundation for a meaningful and well-matched support experience.<br></p><p data-end=\"843\" data-start=\"430\" style=\"margin:0px;color:#10182899\"><strong data-end=\"459\" data-start=\"430\">What this session covers:</strong><br>– Understanding your lifestyle, challenges, and priorities<br>– Exploring support goals (e.g. fitness, confidence, independence, routine)<br>– Identifying preferences, boundaries, and any relevant medical or behavioural considerations<br>– Answering questions about how sessions work and what to expect next<br></p><p data-end=\"1030\" data-start=\"845\" style=\"margin:0px;color:#10182899\"><strong data-end=\"864\" data-start=\"845\">Why it matters:</strong><br>This is a key step in delivering you right support, with the right person, in the right way. After the call, we’ll create a personalised report for your matched provider - so they can tailor sessions to you from day one.</p>",

            "description": "<p style=\"margin:0px;color:#10182899; margin: 0px;color: #10182899;\">Ready to take the next step in your fitness journey? Speak to one of our friendly Fitness Coordinators! </p><p style=\"margin:0px;color:#10182899; margin: 0px;color: #10182899;\"></p><p style=\"margin:0px;color:#10182899; margin: 0px;color: #10182899;\">They'll take the time to understand your unique needs and aspirations, then connect you with a Personal Trainer and/or Active Support Worker that suits your exact requirements.</p><p style=\"margin:0px;color:#10182899; margin: 0px;color: #10182899;\"></p><p style=\"margin:0px;color:#10182899; margin: 0px;color: #10182899;\">Whether you're just starting out or looking to take your fitness to the next level, we've got the perfect match for you.             "deleted": false,
            "description": "<p style=\"margin:0px;color:#10182899\">A personalised 30–45 minute consultation to deeply understand your goals, needs, and preferences. This call is designed to set the foundation for a meaningful and well-matched support experience.</p><p style=\"margin:0px;color:#10182899; margin: 0px;color: #10182899;\" data-start=\"430\" data-end=\"843\"><strong data-start=\"430\" data-end=\"459\">What this session covers:</strong><br>– Understanding your lifestyle, challenges, and priorities<br>– Exploring support goals (e.g. fitness, confidence, independence, routine)<br>– Identifying preferences, boundaries, and any relevant medical or behavioural considerations<br>– Answering questions about how sessions work and what to expect next</p><p style=\"margin:0px;color:#10182899; margin: 0px;color: #10182899;\" data-start=\"845\" data-end=\"1030\"><strong data-start=\"845\" data-end=\"864\">Why it matters:</strong><br>This is a key step in delivering you right support, with the right person, in the right way. After the call, we’ll create a personalised report for your matched provider - so they can tailor sessions to you from day one.</p>",

                "content": "<p><span style=\"color: #000000 !important; color: #000000 !important\">We can't wait to meet you on the call and look forward to assisting you with enquiry.</span></p><p><span style=\"color: #000000 !important; color: #000000 !important\"><strong><em>Team WeFlex 💪🏽</em></strong></span></p><p><a target=\"_blank\" title=\"www.weflex.com.au\" href=\"http://www.weflex.com.au\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\"><span style=\"color: #000000 !important; color: #000000 !important\">www.weflex.com.au</span></a></p><p></p><p><span style=\"color: #000000 !important; color: #000000 !important\">PS - If you haven't had a chance to watch our founding story and the why behind WeFlex, watch it below.</span></p>",

                "content": "<h1 style=\"text-align: center;line-height: 1.15;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); color: rgb(0, 0, 0); color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-family: helvetica, arial, sans-serif\">Your Appointment is Booked</span></h1><p>Hi {{contact.person_first_name}},</p><p><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); color: rgb(0, 0, 0); color: rgb(0, 0, 0)\">Your appointment has been confirmed for {{appointment.only_start_date}} at {{appointment.only_start_time}} {{appointment.timezone}}&nbsp;<br><br>We will give you a call on {{contact.phone}} at the appointment time.</span></p><p><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); color: rgb(0, 0, 0); color: rgb(0, 0, 0)\">If you have to re-schedule for any reason you can do so using this link: </span><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"{{appointment.reschedule_link}}\" title=\"Reschedule\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); color: rgb(0, 0, 0); color: rgb(0, 0, 0)\">Reschedule</span></a></p><p><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); color: rgb(0, 0, 0); color: rgb(0, 0, 0)\">We've also include a couple of handy links for you to add this appointment directly to your calendar.</span></p>",
                "tagName": "mj-text"
            },
